# Awesome TUIs: complete project evidence catalog

Version 1.0.1. This is a first-party documentation survey, not installation testing or a claim of depth saturation.

Inventory: https://github.com/rothgar/awesome-tuis

Pinned list commit: `98a107625c87cca75cd5785afcb61a91e46bb6af`; README SHA-256: `0ddff44f4e23a2abc921aba39d9f48de556e458ba51a6bee4d2ff835c4252f27`.

Each short description below is a bounded quotation from project metadata or the official page, not the Awesome list. Platform names, installation families and documentation topics report literal mentions in the fetched README/page. A mention does not prove support or successful installation. Missing license fields do not establish permission to use the project. Archive flags are GitHub API observations at the stated date. Raw bodies remain private.

- [Dashboards](#dashboards)
